case, we have

\displaystyle Z_{1}(\mu_{n})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{n}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{n}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.10)

Thus by (4.9), (4.8) holds for any
\alpha
(and the corresponding
\mu
) by letting
n\rightarrow\infty
in both sides of (4.10) because the RHS of (4.10) is the partial sum of the RHS of (4.8).

By Lemma 4.3 and (4.8), we have

\displaystyle Z_{1}(\mu)>-\infty

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow-Z_{1}(\mu)<+\infty

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(-\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)<+\infty.

That is,

\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{n+1}-\alpha_{n}|^{2}+|\alpha_{n}|^{4}\Big)<\infty.\qed

Remark 4.6
.

Observing the above argument, by Lemma 4.1, sum rule (4.8) holds for any
\alpha
once it holds for
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. Hence, in what follows, we only prove some sum rules for
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
since they also hold for any
\alpha
by a similar argument to (4.8) from the special case of
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
to general cases at some time.

4.2.
Second order case

Secondly, by using the expressions of
w_{0}
,
w_{1}
and
w_{2}
, we have

Theorem 4.7
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(1-\cos^{2}\theta\big)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\Leftrightarrow\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\left|\alpha_{n+2}-\alpha_{n}\right|^{2}+|\alpha_{n}|^{4}\right)<\infty.

(4.11)

More precisely, for any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(1-\cos^{2}\theta\big)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{8}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right].

(4.12)

Proof.

Denote

Z_{2,1}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos^{2}\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi},

(4.13)

then

Z_{2,1}(\mu)=\frac{1}{2}w_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Re}(w_{2}).

(4.14)

For
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, by (3.25), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(w_{2})=-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\rho_{j}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\big(\alpha_{j}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{2}\big)

in which

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}+\overline{\alpha}_{j+1}\alpha_{j-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\big(\alpha_{j}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{2}\big)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(\alpha_{j}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{2}+\overline{\alpha}_{j}^{2}\alpha_{j-1}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}[(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}+\overline{\alpha}_{j}\alpha_{j-1})^{2}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}[(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}[|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}].

More precisely,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(w_{2})=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big).

(4.15)

By (4.5), (4.14) and (4.15), we have

\displaystyle 2Z_{2,1}(\mu)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|\alpha_{-1}|^{4}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[-\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right].

So

\displaystyle Z_{2,1}(\mu)=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{8}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[-\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right].

(4.16)

For general
\alpha
, noting
Z_{2,1}(\mu)=Z_{Q}(\mu)
with
Q(z)=-\frac{1}{2}(z^{2}-1)
, by a similar argument to (4.8) for any
\alpha
, we get that (4.16) also holds in this case. So (4.12) holds for any
\alpha
.

Now turn to (4.11). Suppose
Z_{2,1}(\mu)>-\infty
, by (4.16), then

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[-\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]<\infty;

(4.17)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<\infty;

(4.18)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<\infty;

(4.19)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}<\infty

(4.20)

and

\hskip-36.98857pt\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}<\infty.

(4.21)

Note that for
a,b\in\mathbb{C}
,

|a-b|^{2}\leq 2(|a|^{2}+|b|^{2}),

(4.22)

setting
a=2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}
and
b=2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}
, by (4.20) and (4.21), we have

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}<\infty.

(4.23)

Since
\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}=|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}
, then

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}=1+2\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-2\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.24)

Thus by (4.18) and (4.23),

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}<\infty.

(4.25)

Furthermore, since
\rho_{j}^{2}=1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}
, by (4.19) and (4.25) (or (4.17)), we get

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<\infty.

(4.26)

That is, the
\Rightarrow
direction in (4.11) is exact.

For the opposite direction, it is easy to know that (4.17)-(4.21) are consequences of (4.25) and (4.26). Thus (4.25) and (4.26) imply
Z_{2,1}(\mu)>-\infty
.
∎

Theorem 4.8
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\Longleftrightarrow\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{n+2}-2\alpha_{n+1}+\alpha_{n}|^{2}+|\alpha_{n}|^{6}\Big)<\infty.

(4.27)

More precisely, for any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{8}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(6|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+6|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{8}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(4|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+4|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.29)

Proof.

Denote

Z_{2,2}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi},

(4.30)

then

Z_{2,2}(\mu)=\frac{3}{2}w_{0}-2\mathrm{Re}(w_{1})+\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Re}(w_{2})=2Z_{1}(\mu)-Z_{2,1}(\mu).

(4.31)

At first, assume that
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. By (4.8), (4.16) and (4.31), we have

\displaystyle Z_{2,2}(\mu)=

\displaystyle\frac{5}{8}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right]

(4.32)

in which

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[2|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.33)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big)+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.34)

Note that

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}=-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big),

(4.35)

then by (4.33)-(4.35), we obtain

\displaystyle Z_{2,2}(\mu)=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{8}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(6|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+6|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.36)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{8}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(4|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+4|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.37)

By using Lemma 4.1 and a similar argument to (4.8) for any
\alpha
, (4.36) and (4.37) also hold for any
\alpha
. That is, (4.28) and (4.29) hold for any any
\alpha
.

By Lemma 4.3, (4.30) and (4.37), we have that

\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty

(4.38)

is equivalent to all of the following conditions

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}<+\infty,

(4.39)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}<+\infty,

(4.40)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty

(4.41)

and

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(4|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+4|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty.

(4.42)

On one hand, it is easy to find that (4.39) and (4.41) imply

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty

(4.43)

since
\rho_{j}\geq\frac{1}{2}
for sufficiently large
j
following from (4.39).

On the other hand, by the following discrete Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality (see
[
2
]
or
[
15
]
)

\|(S-1)\alpha\|_{3}^{2}\leq 2\|(S-1)^{2}\alpha\|_{2}\|\alpha\|_{6},

(4.44)

we know that (4.39) and (4.43) imply

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{3}<+\infty.

(4.45)

By (4.22), triangle inequality and Hölder inequality, we further get (4.40)-(4.42) from (4.39), (4.43) and (4.45).
That is to say that (4.39)-(4.42) is equivalent to (4.39) and (4.43). Thus (4.27) holds.
∎

Remark 4.9
.

Splitting

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.46)

and
noting

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(2|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}+4\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2},

(4.47)

by (4.32), (4.34) and (4.35), we also obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{8}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.48)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{8}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-2\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.49)

Similarly, we can deduce (4.27) from (4.48) and (4.49).

4.3.
Third order case

Next, we apply the expressions of
w_{j}
,
0\leq j\leq 3
to get some some rules, and further obtain some gems under appropriate conditions from these sum rules.
In order to do so, we need the following simple facts on real part of a product of two and three complex variables, some of which have been used in the above two cases such as (4.7).

Proposition 4.10
.

For
A,B,C\in\mathbb{C}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(AB)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(AB+\overline{A}\,\overline{B})=\frac{1}{2}[(A+\overline{A})(B+\overline{B})-(A\overline{B}+\overline{A}B)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(B)-\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B}),

(4.50)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(A\overline{B}+\overline{A}B)=\frac{1}{2}[|A|^{2}+|B|^{2}-(A-B)(\overline{A}-\overline{B})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(|A|^{2}+|B|^{2}-|A-B|^{2})

(4.51)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(ABC)=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(BC)+\mathrm{Re}(B)\mathrm{Re}(AC)+\mathrm{Re}(C)\mathrm{Re}(AB)

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(B)\mathrm{Re}(C)

(4.52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(B)\mathrm{Re}(C)-\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(B\overline{C})-\mathrm{Re}(B)\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C})

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Re}(C)\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B}).

(4.53)

Proof.

(4.50) and (4.51) are trivial. (4.53) follows from (4.50) and (4.52). It is sufficient to get (4.52).
By (4.50), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(ABC)=2\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(BC)-\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B}\,\overline{C}),

(4.54)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B}\,\overline{C})=2\mathrm{Re}(\overline{B})\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C})-\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}\,\overline{B}C)

(4.55)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}\,\overline{B}C)=2\mathrm{Re}(C)\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}\,\overline{B})-\mathrm{Re}(ABC).

(4.56)

Noting that
\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A})=\mathrm{Re}(A)
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C})=2\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(C)-\mathrm{Re}(AC),

then by (4.54)-(4.56), we get

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(ABC)=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(BC)-\mathrm{Re}(\overline{B})\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C})+\mathrm{Re}(C)\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}\,\overline{B})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(BC)+\mathrm{Re}(B)\mathrm{Re}(AC)+\mathrm{Re}(C)\mathrm{Re}(AB)

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}(A)\mathrm{Re}(B)\mathrm{Re}(C).\qed

Denote

Z_{3,1}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 3\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi},

(4.57)

Z_{3,2}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

(4.58)

and

Z_{3,3}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}.

(4.59)

Theorem 4.11
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 3\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}-\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})\Big].

(4.60)

Proof.

Since

1-\cos 3\theta=1-\frac{e^{i3\theta}+e^{-i3\theta}}{2},

then

Z_{3,1}(\mu)=w_{0}-\mathrm{Re}(w_{3}).

(4.61)

By (3.32), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(w_{3})=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+2}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})-\frac{1}{3}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j}^{3}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{3}).

(4.62)

By (4.50), (4.51) and (4.53),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+2}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})=\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}),

(4.63)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{2})=\mathrm{Re}\big((\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})-\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}\overline{\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}),

(4.64)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})=\mathrm{Re}\big((\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j})(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j})\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})-\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j}\overline{\alpha_{j+1}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

(4.65)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j}^{3}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{3})=\mathrm{Re}\big((\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\big(4(\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}))^{2}-3|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{3}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}).

(4.66)

From (4.63),

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+2}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}.

(4.67)

From (4.64),

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}\alpha_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{2})\rho_{j}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2})\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}.

(4.68)

From (4.65),

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j+1}^{2}\overline{\alpha}_{j}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1})\rho_{j}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2})

\displaystyle(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}-(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}.

(4.69)

From (4.66),

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}(\alpha_{j}^{3}\overline{\alpha}_{j-1}^{3})=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{3}

\displaystyle-3|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-3\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\Big].

(4.70)

By (4.61), (4.62), (4.67)-(4.70), we have

\displaystyle Z_{3,1}(\mu)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-3\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}-\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})\Big].\qed

(4.71)

Denote

\displaystyle\mathrm{EP}=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}-\frac{1}{2}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.72)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{CP}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})\Big],

(4.73)

where EP and CP are the meaning that equivalent part and conditional part respectively, then the sum rule in the above theorem (and some similar theorems below) can be expressed as

“Higher order Szegő integral = Equivalent part + Conditional part”.

Straightly speaking, the convergence of higher order Szegő integrals in what follows is equivalent to the convergence of the equivalent part once the conditional part is finite. This is why we call them equivalent part and conditional part respectively. Obviously, the summands in EP are negative and the ones in CP are positive.

Theorem 4.12
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 3\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\Leftrightarrow(S^{3}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.74)

Proof.

Since
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, by Lemma 4.3, Theorem 4.11 and Hölder inequality, the series in CP and EP are finite with only one exception. This exceptional one is

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}.

(4.75)

By the assumption and Hölder inequality again, the second and third series in (4.74) are also finite. So (4.73) follows immediately.
∎

Remark 4.13
.

This is one special case of a result in
[
7
]
due to Golinskii and Zlatoš.

Theorem 4.14
.

Assume
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 3\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\Leftrightarrow\alpha\in\ell^{4}.

(4.76)

Proof.

By triangle inequality, it is easy to know that
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
imply
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
and
(S^{3}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. From these facts and
\alpha\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
, we obtain

\mathrm{CP}<+\infty

(4.77)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 3\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\Leftrightarrow-\mathrm{EP}<\infty.

(4.78)

More precisely,
\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 3\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty
is equivalent to

-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big]<+\infty,

(4.79)

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}<+\infty,

(4.80)

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}<+\infty,

(4.81)

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}<+\infty,

(4.82)

and

\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}<+\infty.

(4.83)

Noting
\rho_{j}<1
, by Hölder inequality and Lemma 4.3, we have that (4.79) implies (4.78) and (4.80)-(4.82). So (4.75) holds by this fact and (4.77) as
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
.
∎

Remark 4.15
.

This is a result similar to one special case of the result in
[
9
]
due to Lukic.

Proposition 4.16
.

For
a,b,c,d\in\mathbb{C}
,

\displaystyle|a-b-c+d|^{2}=

\displaystyle|a-b|^{2}+|a-c|^{2}+|b-d|^{2}+|c-d|^{2}

\displaystyle-|a-d|^{2}-|b-c|^{2}.

(4.84)

Proof.

It follows from direct calculations or parallelogram law. For the latter, more precisely, we have

\displaystyle|a-b-c+d|^{2}+|a-b+c-d|^{2}=2(|a-b|^{2}+|c-d|^{2}),

(4.85)

\displaystyle|a-b+c-d|^{2}+|a+b-c-d|^{2}=2(|a-d|^{2}+|b-c|^{2})

(4.86)

and

\displaystyle|a+b-c-d|^{2}+|a-b-c+d|^{2}=2(|a-c|^{2}+|b-d|^{2}).

(4.87)

Thus, (4.83) immediately follows from (4.84)-(4.86).
∎

Proposition 4.17
.

For any
\alpha=\{\alpha_{j}\}_{0}^{\infty}\in\ell^{2}
and
\alpha_{-1}=-1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=-(|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2})+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.88)

Proof.

By Proposition 4.16, we have

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.89)

for
j\geq 0
. Then for
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
and
\alpha_{-1}=-1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2})+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+2\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.\qed

Theorem 4.18
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}(1+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{12}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}.

(4.90)

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{4}\cos\theta-\frac{1}{2}\cos 2\theta+\frac{1}{4}\cos 3\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}(1-\cos\theta)+\frac{1}{2}(1-\cos 2\theta)-\frac{1}{4}(1-\cos 3\theta),

(4.91)

then

\displaystyle Z_{3,2}(\mu)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}w_{0}-\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Re}(w_{1})-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Re}(w_{2})+\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Re}(w_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}Z_{1}(\mu)+Z_{2,1}(\mu)-\frac{1}{4}Z_{3,1}(\mu).

(4.92)

For
\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, by (4.2), (4.12), (4.60), (4.87) and (4.91), we have

\displaystyle Z_{3,2}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big)-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6}+\frac{1}{8}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle-\Big\{\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}(1+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{12}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}.\qed

As before, the equivalent part and conditional part in this case are as follows:

\displaystyle\mathrm{EP}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}(1+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{12}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

(4.93)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{CP}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}.

(4.94)

Applying sum rule (4.89), we obtain a series of higher order analogues of Szegő theorem under appropriate conditions.

Theorem 4.19
.

Assume
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\,\,(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}\,\,and\,\,(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.95)

Proof.

By (4.88) (or directly (4.87)), we have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+1)(|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.96)

Noting that

\displaystyle||\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|(|\alpha_{j+1}|+|\alpha_{j-1}|)

(4.97)

and

\displaystyle\big||\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|),

(4.98)

where triangle inequality
\big||a|-|b|\big|\leq|a-b|
is used, by the assumption, (4.95) and using Hölder inequality (or Young inequality), the series in (4.89) are convergent except two ones in EP, more precisely,

-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

and

-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}.

That is to say that
Z_{3,2}(\mu)>-\infty
is equivalent to
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}
and
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
as
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
.
∎

Corollary 4.20
.

Assume
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}.

(4.99)

Proof.

It is easy to know that
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
implies
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. Thus (4.98) follows from (4.94).
∎

Remark 4.21
.

This is the main result of Lukic in
[
8
]
, which disproves the original conjecture of Simon in
[
12
]
.

Corollary 4.22
.

Assume
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.100)

Proof.

The key is to note that
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
implies
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}
.
∎

With respect to one-directional implication from
\alpha
to
Z_{3,2}(\mu)
, we have

Corollary 4.23
.

Assume
(S-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

Proof.

By (4.44),
(S-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
imply
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
, further
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, whereas
(S-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
implies
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
.
∎

Corollary 4.24
.

Assume
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
,
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
,
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

Proof.

This is an immediate consequence of Theorem 4.19.
∎

Corollary 4.25
.

Assume
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
,
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

Proof.

Note that
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
implies
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}
.
∎

Corollary 4.26
.

Assume
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
,
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{4}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

Proof.

Applying (4.44) to
(S+1)\alpha
,
(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
and
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
imply
(S^{2}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
(also see Theorem 2.5 in
[
2
]
).
∎

Remark 4.27
.

Corollary 4.26 is an important result in
[
2
]
which provides one half support for the validity of Lukic conjecture according to the view of Breuer, Simon and Zeitouni.

Moreover, we have

Theorem 4.28
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S-1)^{2}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.101)

Proof.

By the assumpation,
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
and the series in EP are convergent except

-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

Thus (4.100) holds.
∎

Remark 4.29
.

This is one special case of a result in
[
7
]
due to Golinskii and Zlatoš.

Theorem 4.30
.

Assume
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{6}.

(4.102)

Proof.

By the assumpation,
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
. Therefore,
Z_{3,2}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
-\mathrm{EP}<+\infty
. By the assumption, Lemma 4.3 and Hölder inequality, it is easy to get that
-\mathrm{EP}<+\infty
is equivalent to
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
.
∎

Remark 4.31
.

As Theorem 4.14, this is also a result similar to one special case of the result in
[
9
]
due to Lukic .

Theorem 4.32
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{23}{12}+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{3}{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}+\frac{5}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-3\alpha_{j+1}+3\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-\frac{5}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big].

(4.103)

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle(1-\cos\theta)^{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{5}{2}-\frac{15}{4}\cos\theta+\frac{3}{2}\cos 2\theta-\frac{1}{4}\cos 3\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{15}{4}(1-\cos\theta)-\frac{3}{2}(1-\cos 2\theta)+\frac{1}{4}(1-\cos 3\theta),

then

\displaystyle Z_{3,3}(\mu)=\frac{15}{4}Z_{1}(\mu)-3Z_{2,1}(\mu)+\frac{1}{4}Z_{3,1}(\mu).

(4.104)

By (4.2), (4.12), (4.60) and (4.103), we obtain

\displaystyle Z_{3,3}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{15}{8}+\frac{15}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big)-\frac{15}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{8}-\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{8}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]\rho_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{23}{12}+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{3}{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}+\frac{5}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-3\alpha_{j+1}+3\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-\frac{5}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big],

where the following identity is used

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+2}-3\alpha_{j+1}+3\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-3|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+9|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-3|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+3|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.105)

which implying

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-3\alpha_{j+1}+3\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{15}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{3}{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.106)

∎

As before, set

\displaystyle\mathrm{EP}=

\displaystyle\frac{23}{12}+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}-\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{3}{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}|\alpha_{1}+1|^{2}+\frac{5}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-3\alpha_{j+1}+3\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}-\frac{5}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

(4.107)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{CP}=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big].

(4.108)

By using sum rule (4.102), we have

Theorem 4.33
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S-1)^{3}\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.109)

Proof.

By the assumption, it is easy to get that
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
and almost all series in
\mathrm{EP}
are convergent except only one, that is,

-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-3\alpha_{j+1}+3\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

Thus
Z_{3,3}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
(S-1)^{3}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
as
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
.
∎

Remark 4.34
.

This is also one special case of a result in
[
7
]
due to Golinskii and Zlatoš.

Theorem 4.35
.

Assume
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{1}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{8}.

(4.110)

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle 2(a^{3}+b^{3}+c^{3})-2abc-ab(a+b)-bc(b+c)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(a^{3}+b^{3}+c^{3})-(a+b+c)(a+c)b

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(a^{3}+c^{3})-(a+b+c)(a+c-2b)b-2b^{2}(a+c)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2a(a^{2}-b^{2})+2c(c^{2}-b^{2})-(a+b+c)(a+c-2b)b

(4.111)

and

\displaystyle\big||a|^{2}-|b|^{2}\big|\leq|a-b|(|a|+|b|),

(4.112)

where
a,b,c\in\mathbb{C}
.

Let
a=|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}
,
b=|\alpha_{j}|^{2}
and
c=|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}
, by (4.110), we have

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\big(1-|\alpha_{0}|^{6}\big)-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}\big)+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\big(1-|\alpha_{0}|^{6}\big)-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big)-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\big(1-|\alpha_{0}|^{6}\big)-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|-|\alpha_{j}|\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|+|\alpha_{j}|\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j-1}|-|\alpha_{j}|\big)\big(|\alpha_{j-1}|+|\alpha_{j}|\big)\big(|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|-|\alpha_{j}|\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|+|\alpha_{j}|\big)|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j-1}|-|\alpha_{j}|\big)\big(|\alpha_{j-1}|+|\alpha_{j}|\big)|\alpha_{j}|^{2}.

(4.113)

By the assumption, (4.111), (4.112) and triangle inequality, we obtain that the series in (4.102) are finite except

\frac{5}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+\frac{1}{3}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}\Big).

So by Lemma 4.3,
Z_{3,3}(\mu)>-\infty
is equivalent to
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
as
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{1}
.
∎

Remark 4.36
.

Noting the following Young inequality,

|a-b||c|^{2}|d|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}|a-b|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}(|c|^{8}+|d|^{8}),

(4.114)

where
a,b,c\in\mathbb{C}
, by (4.102), (4.112) and (4.113), it is easy to get that

\displaystyle(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}\,\,\mathrm{and}\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{8}\,\,\Longrightarrow\,\,\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

(4.115)

Remark 4.37
.

For the case of
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{1}
, we mainly used the identity (4.110) and the inequality (4.111) to treat the intractable series in (4.112) in the proof of the above theorem. Indeed, an alternate approach is to apply the following telescoping sum

\displaystyle A_{1}A_{2}\cdots A_{n}-B_{1}B_{2}\cdots B_{n}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}A_{1}\cdots A_{j-1}(A_{j}-B_{j})B_{j+1}\cdots B_{n}.

(4.116)

Such idea was used in
[
9
]
.

Corresponding to the above theorem and (4.114), we actually have a stronger result as follows

Theorem 4.38
.

Assume
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{8}.

(4.117)

Proof.

Since (see
[
9
]
)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{z_{1}^{3}+z_{2}^{3}+z_{3}^{3}}{3}-z_{1}z_{2}z_{3}\right|\leq 4\max_{1\leq i,j\leq 3}|z_{i}-z_{j}|^{2},

(4.118)

where
z_{1},z_{2},z_{3}\in\overline{\mathbb{D}}
, then

\displaystyle\left|\frac{|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\max_{k,l\in\{-1,0,1\}}|\alpha_{j+k}-\alpha_{j+l}|^{2},

(4.119)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\right|\leq 16\max_{k,l\in\{0,1\}}|\alpha_{j+k}-\alpha_{j+l}|^{2},

(4.120)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\right|\leq 16\max_{k,l\in\{0,1\}}|\alpha_{j+k}-\alpha_{j+l}|^{2},

(4.121)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\right|\leq 16\max_{k,l\in\{-1,0\}}|\alpha_{j+k}-\alpha_{j+l}|^{2}

(4.122)

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\right|\leq 16\max_{k,l\in\{-1,0\}}|\alpha_{j+k}-\alpha_{j+l}|^{2},

(4.123)

where we have used the following inequality

\big||a|^{2}-|b|^{2}\big|^{2}\leq 4|a-b|^{2}

for any
a,b\in\overline{\mathbb{D}}
.
In addition, we have

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}=

\displaystyle\frac{5}{24}\big(|\alpha_{0}|^{6}-1\big)+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\frac{|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big(\frac{2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{6}}{3}+\frac{|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{6}}{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{2|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}+\frac{|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}\Big)\Big].

(4.124)

By (4.118)-(4.123), we get that the series in (4.112) is finite as
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. Thus by (4.102), we obtain that
Z_{3,3}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
as
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
.
∎

Remark 4.39
.

This is one special case of the result in
[
9
]
due to Lukic. The idea for the argument was also used in it. In this special case, the only difference is that we have the explicit sum rule (4.102) whereas there was no such sum rule in
[
9
]
. Such idea is based on the following inequality due to Lukic (for details, see Lemma 2.2 in
[
9
]
)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{z_{1}^{n}+z_{2}^{n}+\cdots+z_{n}^{n}}{n}-z_{1}z_{2}\cdots z_{n}\right|\leq(n-1)^{2}\max_{1\leq i,j\leq n}|z_{i}-z_{j}|^{2},

(4.125)

where
z_{1},z_{2},\ldots,z_{n}\in\overline{\mathbb{D}}
. As
n=3
, (4.124) is just (4.117). In what follows, we will use this inequality for many times.

Remark 4.40
.

From the arguments to Theorems 4.35 and 4.38, the roles of equivalent and conditional parts are not absolute for some assumptions (for instance,
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{1}
in Theorem 4.35 and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
in Theorem 4.38) because some summands in both EP and CP should be jointly considered for applying of the assumption (for example, in the above two theorems,
-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|
is in EP whereas
\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}
and
\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}
are in CP). Nevertheless, the roles of them are worthy of their names in most situations.

4.4.
Fourth order case

Finally, as the end of this section, we establish some sum rules of fourth order concerning with
w_{j}
,
0\leq j\leq 4
.
Set

Z_{4,1}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 4\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi},

(4.126)

Z_{4,2}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi},

(4.127)

Z_{4,3}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

(4.128)

and

Z_{4,4}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{4}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}.

(4.129)

By using (4.50), we can extend (4.50) and (4.52) to more general cases.

Proposition 4.41
.

(1)

For any even
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n})=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=n+1}^{2n}(-1)^{2n-l}\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{l-1}\overline{A}_{l+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{l})

\displaystyle+\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n})\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}_{n+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n});

(4.130)

(2)

For any odd
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n})=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=n+2}^{2n}(-1)^{2n-l}\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{l-1}\overline{A}_{l+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{l})

\displaystyle+\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n+1})\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}_{n+2}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n});

(4.131)

(3)

For any
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}A_{2}\cdots A_{2n-1})=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{2n-1}(-1)^{2n-1-l}\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{l-1}\overline{A}_{l+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n-1})\mathrm{Re}(A_{l}),

(4.132)

where
A_{l}\in\mathbb{C}
,
1\leq l\leq 2n
.

Proof.

Since the arguments are similar, it is sufficient for us to deduce the case (1).

By (4.50),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n})=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-1})\mathrm{Re}(A_{2n})

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-1}\overline{A}_{2n}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-1}\overline{A}_{2n})=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-2}\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{2n-1})

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n-1}\overline{A}_{2n}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\overline{A}_{n+2}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}\overline{A}_{n+2}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{n+1})

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}\overline{A}_{n+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n-1}\overline{A}_{2n})

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}\overline{A}_{n+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n})\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}_{n+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n}),

then we get

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-1})\mathrm{Re}(A_{2n})-2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-2}\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{2n-1})

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n-3}\overline{A}_{2n-1}\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{2n-2})-\cdots

\displaystyle+(-1)^{2n-l}2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{l-1}\overline{A}_{l+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n-1}\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{l})+\cdots

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}\overline{A}_{n+2}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})\mathrm{Re}(A_{n+1})+2\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n})\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A}_{n+1}\cdots\overline{A}_{2n})

\displaystyle-\mathrm{Re}(A_{1}\cdots A_{n}A_{n+1}\cdots A_{2n}).

Thus (4.129) immediately holds.
∎

Remark 4.42
.

From (4.50), (4.52), (4.129), (4.130) and (4.131), applying recursion step by step, we know that any real part of a product of
n
complex numbers can be explicitly expressed in terms of the real parts of one complex and products of two complex numbers.

Similarly, we also have

Proposition 4.43
.

For
A,B,C,D\in\mathbb{C}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(AB\overline{C}\,\overline{D})=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C})\mathrm{Re}(B\overline{D})+\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{D})\mathrm{Re}(B\overline{C})-\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B})\mathrm{Re}(C\overline{D}).

(4.133)

Proof.

By (4.50), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(AB\overline{C}\,\overline{D})=2\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C})\mathrm{Re}(B\overline{D})-\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C}\,\overline{B}D),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{C}\,\overline{B}D)=2\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B})\mathrm{Re}(\overline{C}D)-\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B}C\overline{D})

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{B}C\overline{D})=2\mathrm{Re}(A\overline{D})\mathrm{Re}(\overline{B}C)-\mathrm{Re}(AB\overline{C}\,\overline{D}),

Thus (4.132) follows these facts and
\mathrm{Re}(A)=\mathrm{Re}(\overline{A})
for any
A\in\mathbb{C}
.
∎

Theorem 4.44
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 4\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+2}^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]
