\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(1+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(1+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big].\qed

By virtue of sum rule (4.133), we can easily obtain the following higher order Szegő theorems. Here and in some other sum rules below (i.e., (4.157), (4.167) and (4.176)),
\mathrm{EP}
and
\mathrm{CP}
are analogously defined as before. That is,
\mathrm{EP}
is the sum of negative series in sum rules whereas
\mathrm{CP}
is the sum of positive series in them.

Theorem 4.45
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 4\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{4}.

(4.145)

Remark 4.46
.

This is a weaker result similar to the one in
[
9
]
due to Lukic .

Proof.

Since
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, by Young inequality (or Hölder inequality), then
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
. Moreover,
the series in
\mathrm{EP}
are convergent except the following ones

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big),

(4.146)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}),

(4.147)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}),

(4.148)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}),

(4.149)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

(4.150)

and

-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big).

(4.151)

Thus
Z_{4,1}(\mu)>-\infty
is equivalent to all above series are convergent in (4.145)-(4.150) under the assumption of
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. By Lemma 4.3 and Hölder inequality, it is easy to see that this fact means that

Z_{4,1}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}<+\infty
(that is,
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
) as
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
.
∎

Theorem 4.47
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 4\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S^{4}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.152)

Remark 4.48
.

This is one special case of the result in
[
7
]
due to Golinskii and Zlatoš.

Proof.

By sum rule (4.133), as
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, all the series in
\mathrm{CP}
and the ones in
\mathrm{EP}
are finite except

-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+2}^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.153)

Then
Z_{4,1}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty
(namely,
(S^{4}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
) as
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
.
∎

Theorem 4.49
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos 4\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S^{4}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}\,\,and\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{4}.

(4.154)

Remark 4.50
.

This is a one-case result of the original Simon conjecture under certain conditions.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle|a|^{2}|b|^{2}|c|^{2}\leq\frac{|a|^{6}+|b|^{6}+|c|^{6}}{3}

(4.155)

and (by Young inequality)

\displaystyle|a|^{2}|b-c|^{2}\leq\frac{|a|^{6}}{3}+\frac{3|b-c|^{3}}{2}

(4.156)

for any
a,b,c\in\mathbb{C}
,
then by (4.133),
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
and the series in
\mathrm{EP}
are finite except the ones in (4.145)-(4.150) and the following one

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+2}^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.157)

Thus
Z_{4,1}(\mu)>-\infty
is equivalent to
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}<+\infty
and
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty
(i.e.,
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
and
(S^{4}-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
) as
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
.
∎

Theorem 4.51
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{32}+\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{5}{32}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{3}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{2}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{3}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+1}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{4}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+3|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]\Big\}.

(4.158)

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{8}-\frac{1}{2}\cos 2\theta+\frac{1}{8}\cos 4\theta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(1-\cos 2\theta)-\frac{1}{8}(1-\cos 4\theta),

(4.159)

then

Z_{4,2}(\mu)=\frac{3}{8}w_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Re}(w_{2})+\frac{1}{8}\mathrm{Re}(w_{4})=Z_{2,1}(\mu)-\frac{1}{8}Z_{4,1}(\mu).

(4.160)

By (4.12), (4.133a) and (4.159), we have

\displaystyle Z_{4,2}(\mu)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\big(1-\cos^{2}\theta\big)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{8}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left[\big(2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+2}^{2}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle-\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{4}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}

\displaystyle-4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{9}{32}+\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{5}{32}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{3}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{2}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{3}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+1}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{4}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+3|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(2|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]\Big\},

where the following identity is used

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+1}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=2|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

which implying

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+1}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}.\qed

Theorem 4.52
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S^{2}-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.161)

Remark 4.53
.

This is also a one-case result of the original Simon conjecture under certain conditions.

Proof.

Since

\big(|a|^{2}-|b|^{2}\big)^{2}=\big(|a|+|b|\big)^{2}\big(|a|-|b|\big)^{2}\leq 2\big(|a|^{2}+|b|^{2}\big)|a-b|^{2}

(4.162)

for any
a,b\in\mathbb{C}
, by applying (4.154) and (4.155) to the sum rule (4.157), we have that if
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
, then
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
and the series in
\mathrm{EP}
are convergent except the following one

-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+1}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.163)

So
Z_{4,2}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+1}+\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty
(i.e.,
(S^{2}-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
) as
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
.
∎

Theorem 4.54
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S^{2}-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.164)

Remark 4.55
.

This is one special case of a result in
[
7
]
due to Golinskii and Zlatoš.

Proof.

As
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, by the sum rule (4.157) and Hölder inequality, we have that the series in it are convergent except the only one (4.162). Thus
Z_{4,2}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
(S^{2}-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
as
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
.

(4.165)

∎

Theorem 4.56
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{6}.

(4.166)

Proof.

As
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, by (4.161) and the sum rule (4.157), we have
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
. So
Z_{4,2}(\mu)>-\infty
is equivalent to
-\mathrm{EP}<+\infty
under the assumption of
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
. By applying (4.154) to
\mathrm{EP}
, it is easy to know that
-\mathrm{EP}<+\infty
is equivalent to
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
.
∎

Remark 4.57
.

This is a weaker result similar to the one of Lukic in
[
9
]
.

With respect to one-directional implication from
\alpha
to
Z_{4,2}(\mu)
, we have

Theorem 4.58
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{2}(1+\cos\theta)^{2}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

(4.167)

Proof.

As a direct proof, similar to Theorem 4.52, it mainly follows from (4.154) and (4.155). As an indirect proof, note that
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
implies
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and

(S^{2}-1)^{2}\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, it is a consequence of Theorem 4.52. More immediately, it is also a consequence of Theorem 4.56.
∎

Theorem 4.59
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{37}{96}-\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{2}|^{2}(|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+|\alpha_{1}|^{2})-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{7}{32}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{1}|^{4}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|1+\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}|1+\alpha_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+2}+2\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\Big\{-\frac{1}{3}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{8}+4\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(1+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\Big\},

where the following identity is used

\displaystyle|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+2}+2\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}-2|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+4|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-2|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

which implying

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+2}+2\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{bdy}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

with boundary

\displaystyle\mathrm{bdy}=-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}|1+\alpha_{1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|1+\alpha_{2}|^{2}.\,\,\,\,\hskip 17.07164pt\qed

Theorem 4.60
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S-1)^{3}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.171)

Remark 4.61
.

This is still a one-case result of the original Simon conjecture under certain conditions.

Proof.

By applying (4.154), (4.155) and (4.161) to the sum rule (4.167), we have that if
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
, then
\mathrm{CP}<+\infty
and the series in
\mathrm{EP}
are convergent except the following one

-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+2}+2\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}.

(4.172)

So
Z_{4,3}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
|\alpha_{j+3}-2\alpha_{j+2}+2\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}<+\infty
(i.e.,
(S-1)^{3}(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
) as
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
.
∎

Theorem 4.62
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,(S-1)^{3}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}.

(4.173)

Remark 4.63
.

This is one special case of a result in
[
7
]
due to Golinskii and Zlatoš.

Proof.

As
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
, by the sum rule (4.167) and Hölder inequality, we have that the series in it are convergent except the only one (4.171). Thus
Z_{4,3}(\mu)>-\infty
if and only if
(S-1)^{3}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
as
\alpha\in\ell^{4}
.
∎

Theorem 4.64
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty\,\,\Longleftrightarrow\,\,\alpha\in\ell^{6}.

(4.174)

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Big\{-\frac{1}{3}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{24}+\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{4}-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{2}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{2}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}-\frac{1}{48}|\alpha_{0}|^{6}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}-|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\big)|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{|\alpha_{j+2}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{|\alpha_{j+2}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}}{3}-|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\right),

(4.175)

then if
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, by (4.113), (4.117), (4.161) and the sum rule (4.167), we have the series in
\mathrm{CP}
and
\mathrm{EP}
are finite except the following one

\displaystyle\frac{5}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big(\log(1-|\alpha_{j}|^{2})+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big)

and
\displaystyle{-\frac{1}{24}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{6}}
in (4.174). Thus, by Lemma 4.3, we get that

Z_{4,3}(\mu)>-\infty
is equivalent to
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
as
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
.
∎

Remark 4.65
.

As Theorem 4.56, this is also a weaker result similar to the one in
[
9
]
due to Lukic.

As Theorem 4.58, we have

Theorem 4.66
.

Assume
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
and
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{3}(1+\cos\theta)\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}>-\infty.

(4.176)

Proof.

For a direct proof, similar to Theorem 4.60, it mainly follows from (4.154) and (4.155). For an indirect proof, note that
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
implies
(S-1)\alpha\in\ell^{3}
and
(S-1)^{3}(S+1)\alpha\in\ell^{2}
, it is a consequence of Theorem 4.60. More immediately, it is a consequence of Theorem 4.64 since
\alpha\in\ell^{6}
implies
\alpha\in\ell^{8}
.
∎

Theorem 4.67
.

For any
\alpha
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}(1-\cos\theta)^{4}\log w(\theta)\frac{d\theta}{2\pi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{653}{192}-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}+\frac{25}{32}|\alpha_{0}|^{4}-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{1}|^{4}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{0}|^{2}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{2}|^{2}|\alpha_{1}|^{2}+\frac{5}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{16}|\alpha_{0}|^{6}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{j=0}^{3}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{1}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}|\alpha_{0}+1|^{2}

\displaystyle+\Big\{-\frac{15}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}-|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big]|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+3}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j+1}^{2}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\big(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)\big(|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\big)

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho_{j}^{4}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[\big(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)^{2}+\frac{7}{8}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}(|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\big(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big)|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[(|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+2|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})|\alpha_{j+1}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2})(|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{4})\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\big(|\alpha_{j+3}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{6}+|\alpha_{j}|^{4}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Big[|\alpha_{j}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}-\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}\Big[|\alpha_{j+2}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j-1}|^{4}+|\alpha_{j+1}|^{2}|\alpha_{j-1}|^{2}+|\alpha_{j+2}|^{2}|\alpha_{j}|^{2}
